The universe is not asking you to become someone else. It is asking you to stop abandoning who you truly are. You are walking back into alignment.

The moment you choose peace over panic, you begin reclaiming your own divine authority. Trust does not require seeing the whole road; it asks only that you honour the next true step. Growth begins when the soul becomes louder than the fear. Wisdom does not always shout; often it waits patiently beneath reaction. The universe is not asking you to become someone else. It is asking you to stop abandoning who you truly are. You are walking back into alignment.
